53	def find_e1_reports():
54	    """Return the path to the E1-REPORTS folder under the Drive mount, or None if not present yet."""
55	    for r in _candidate_roots():
56	        cand = os.path.join(r, FOLDER)
57	        if os.path.isdir(cand):
58	            return cand
59	    # last resort: a shallow glob for the folder name on any mounted drive
60	    for letter in string.ascii_uppercase[3:]:
61	        for hit in glob.glob(letter + ":\\**\\" + FOLDER, recursive=False):
62	            if os.path.isdir(hit):
63	                return hit
64	    return None
65	
66	
67	def _read_state():
68	    import json
69	    try:
70	        return json.load(open(STATE, encoding="utf-8"))
71	    except Exception:
72	        return {}
73	
74	
75	def _write_state(d):
76	    import json
77	    try:
78	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(STATE), exist_ok=True)
79	        json.dump(d, open(STATE, "w", encoding="utf-8"))
80	    except Exception:
81	        pass
82	
83	
84	def mirror(notify_fn=None):
85	    """Copy the status bundle into E1-REPORTS. Returns (copied_count, dest_path) or (None, None) if the
86	    folder is not there yet (in which case it 🙋's the install step at most once per REMIND_HOURS)."""
87	    dest = find_e1_reports()
88	    if dest is None:
89	        st = _read_state()
90	        now = time.time()
91	        if (now - st.get("last_remind_ts", 0)) / 3600.0 >= REMIND_HOURS:
92	            (notify_fn or _notify)(INSTALL)
93	            st["last_remind_ts"] = now
94	            _write_state(st)
95	        return None, None
96	    copied = 0
97	    for src in FILES:
98	        try:
99	            if os.path.exists(src):
100	                shutil.copy2(src, os.path.join(dest, os.path.basename(src)))
101	                copied += 1
102	        except Exception:
103	            continue
104	    return copied, dest
105	
106	
107	def _notify(msg):
108	    try:
109	        from scripts.notify import notify
110	        notify(msg)
111	    except Exception:
112	        pass
113	
114	
115	if __name__ == "__main__":
116	    from scripts import status_reports
117	    status_reports.build_all()                         # freshen the three generated artifacts first
118	    c, d = mirror()
119	    if d:
120	        print("MIRRORED %d files -> %s" % (c, d))
121	    else:
122	        print("E1-REPORTS folder NOT found under any Drive mount — 🙋 install step sent (retries every 5 min).")
123	        print("Searched:", ", ".join(_candidate_roots()[:6]), "...")

tool_result: 140	def _minute_specs(today_iso):
141	    """≤60-day minute slices 2026-07-15 → today, per instrument (Kite's minute range cap)."""
142	    from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
143	    start = datetime.fromisoformat(SINCE).date()
144	    end = datetime.fromisoformat(today_iso).date()
145	    chunks = []
146	    a = start
147	    while a <= end:
148	        b = min(a + timedelta(days=55), end)
149	        chunks.append((a.isoformat(), b.isoformat()))
150	        a = b + timedelta(days=1)
151	    specs = []
152	    for tok in ds.frozen_tokens():
153	        for frm, to in chunks:
154	            specs.append((str(tok), "minute", frm, to))
155	    return specs

auto-revert if it can't. Runs after build hours as a scheduled job under START_E1_STACK's schedule (no new
process of its own). The 08:15 login gate stays as the SECOND check.

SAFETY (owner ruling 2026-09-22): NO restart between 05:30 and 15:45 — the 06:00 login is the restart. If the
job fires in that window it SKIPS the restart and returns "skipped". restart-is-human (R-NIGHT-CLOSE) is
honoured by this being the one sanctioned automated restart with a revert net (lead-checklist rule 10).

Flow: tag the last verified commit → restart_stack.run() → wait one status cycle → verify (processes up,
public page fresh, NOW line moving). PASS → "NIGHT DRY RUN PASS <commit>". FAIL → revert to the tag, restart
with it, "NIGHT DRY RUN FAILED — reverted to <tag>: <reason>" + a LEAD-RULING-NEEDED flag.

Every heavy dependency is injectable so the logic is tested without touching the live stack or any ledger.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime
import os
import subprocess

REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))


def _ist_now():
    return datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone(datetime.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))


def in_safety_window(now=None):
    """True inside 05:30–15:45 IST — the no-restart window (SAFETY ruling)."""
    now = now or _ist_now()
    hm = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
    return 5 * 60 + 30 <= hm <= 15 * 60 + 45


def _head_commit():
    try:
        return subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"], cwd=REPO,
                              capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
    except Exception:
        return "?"
